SQL INSERT INTO ab_raw
            WITH seg AS MATERIALIZED (
              SELECT ANY_VALUE(b) b, ANY_VALUE(frm) frm, ANY_VALUE(t2) t2, ANY_VALUE(v) v
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 8 = 0
                AND (frm IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) OR t2 IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs))
              GROUP BY tag, tx, li),
            d AS (
              SELECT t2 AS a, b, CAST(v AS HUGEINT) AS d FROM seg
              UNION ALL
              SELECT frm, b, -CAST(v AS HUGEINT) FROM seg WHERE frm <> '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000')
            SELECT a, b, SUM(d) FROM d WHERE a IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) GROUP BY a, b

SQL INSERT INTO ab_raw
            WITH seg AS MATERIALIZED (
              SELECT ANY_VALUE(b) b, ANY_VALUE(frm) frm, ANY_VALUE(t2) t2, ANY_VALUE(v) v
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 8 = 1
                AND (frm IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) OR t2 IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs))
              GROUP BY tag, tx, li),
            d AS (
              SELECT t2 AS a, b, CAST(v AS HUGEINT) AS d FROM seg
              UNION ALL
              SELECT frm, b, -CAST(v AS HUGEINT) FROM seg WHERE frm <> '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000')
            SELECT a, b, SUM(d) FROM d WHERE a IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) GROUP BY a, b

CATALOG {"tables": [["ab"], ["only_addrs"]], "views": [["raw_rows"]]}
SQL SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ab
SQL CREATE TABLE peaks AS
            WITH cum AS (SELECT a, b, SUM(dd) OVER (PARTITION BY a ORDER BY b) c FROM ab),
                 mx AS (SELECT a, MAX(c) mc FROM cum GROUP BY a HAVING MAX(c) > 0)
            SELECT m.a, m.mc, MIN(cum.b) pb FROM mx m
            JOIN cum ON cum.a = m.a AND cum.c = m.mc GROUP BY m.a, m.mc
SQL SELECT a, mc, pb FROM peaks

CATALOG {"tables": [["ab_raw"], ["only_addrs"]], "views": [["raw_rows"]]}
SQL SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ab_raw
SQL CREATE TABLE ab AS SELECT a, b, SUM(dd) dd FROM ab_raw GROUP BY a, b
SQL DROP TABLE ab_raw
CATALOG {"tables": [["ab"], ["only_addrs"]], "views": [["raw_rows"]]}
SQL SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ab
SQL CREATE TABLE peaks AS
            WITH cum AS (SELECT a, b, SUM(dd) OVER (PARTITION BY a ORDER BY b) c FROM ab),
                 mx AS (SELECT a, MAX(c) mc FROM cum GROUP BY a HAVING MAX(c) > 0)
            SELECT m.a, m.mc, MIN(cum.b) pb FROM mx m
            JOIN cum ON cum.a = m.a AND cum.c = m.mc GROUP BY m.a, m.mc
SQL SELECT a, mc, pb FROM peaks

SQL INSERT INTO ab_raw
            WITH seg AS MATERIALIZED (
              SELECT ANY_VALUE(b) b, ANY_VALUE(frm) frm, ANY_VALUE(t2) t2, ANY_VALUE(v) v
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 8 = 0
                AND (frm IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) OR t2 IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs))
              GROUP BY tag, tx, li),
            d AS (
              SELECT t2 AS a, b, CAST(v AS HUGEINT) AS d FROM seg
              UNION ALL
              SELECT frm, b, -CAST(v AS HUGEINT) FROM seg WHERE frm <> '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000')
            SELECT a, b, SUM(d) FROM d WHERE a IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) GROUP BY a, b

SQL WITH g AS (
              SELECT tag, tx, li, COUNT(*) n, COUNT(DISTINCT (b, ts, frm, t2, v)) variants
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 8 = 1 GROUP BY tag, tx, li)
            SELECT COALESCE(SUM(n), 0), COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE variants > 1) FROM g
SQL INSERT INTO ab_raw
            WITH seg AS MATERIALIZED (
              SELECT ANY_VALUE(b) b, ANY_VALUE(frm) frm, ANY_VALUE(t2) t2, ANY_VALUE(v) v
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 8 = 1
                AND (frm IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) OR t2 IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs))
              GROUP BY tag, tx, li),
            d AS (
              SELECT t2 AS a, b, CAST(v AS HUGEINT) AS d FROM seg
              UNION ALL
              SELECT frm, b, -CAST(v AS HUGEINT) FROM seg WHERE frm <> '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000')
            SELECT a, b, SUM(d) FROM d WHERE a IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) GROUP BY a, b

CATALOG {"tables": [["ab"], ["only_addrs"]], "views": [["raw_rows"]]}
SQL SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ab
SQL CREATE TABLE peaks AS
            WITH cum AS (SELECT a, b, SUM(dd) OVER (PARTITION BY a ORDER BY b) c FROM ab),
                 mx AS (SELECT a, MAX(c) mc FROM cum GROUP BY a HAVING MAX(c) > 0)
            SELECT m.a, m.mc, MIN(cum.b) pb FROM mx m
            JOIN cum ON cum.a = m.a AND cum.c = m.mc GROUP BY m.a, m.mc
SQL SELECT a, mc, pb FROM peaks

SQL INSERT INTO ab_raw
            WITH seg AS MATERIALIZED (
              SELECT ANY_VALUE(b) b, ANY_VALUE(frm) frm, ANY_VALUE(t2) t2, ANY_VALUE(v) v
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 1 = 0
                AND (frm IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) OR t2 IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs))
              GROUP BY tag, tx, li),
            d AS (
              SELECT t2 AS a, b, CAST(v AS HUGEINT) AS d FROM seg
              UNION ALL
              SELECT frm, b, -CAST(v AS HUGEINT) FROM seg WHERE frm <> '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000')
            SELECT a, b, SUM(d) FROM d WHERE a IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) GROUP BY a, b

SQL SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ab
SQL CREATE TABLE peaks AS
            WITH cum AS (SELECT a, b, SUM(dd) OVER (PARTITION BY a ORDER BY b) c FROM ab),
                 mx AS (SELECT a, MAX(c) mc FROM cum GROUP BY a HAVING MAX(c) > 0)
            SELECT m.a, m.mc, MIN(cum.b) pb FROM mx m
            JOIN cum ON cum.a = m.a AND cum.c = m.mc GROUP BY m.a, m.mc

SQL CREATE VIEW raw_rows AS 
        SELECT l.block_number b,
               COALESCE(strftime(make_timestamp((bt.ts_i * 1000000)::BIGINT), '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S'), '') ts,
               lower(l.transaction_hash) tx, l.log_index li,
               '0x' || right(lower(COALESCE(l.topic1, repeat('0', 64))), 40) frm,
               '0x' || right(lower(COALESCE(l.topic2, repeat('0', 64))), 40) t2,
               CASE WHEN l.data IS NULL OR l.data IN ('', '0x') THEN '0' ELSE CAST(('0x'||substr(data,35,16))::UBIGINT::HUGEINT * '18446744073709551616'::HUGEINT + ('0x'||substr(data,51,16))::UBIGINT::HUGEINT AS VARCHAR) END v,
               't' tag
        FROM read_parquet('/private/var/folders/z6/mlppyd097493blf6v8_h29dw0000gp/T/w1-zw0vnb4p/main/v2/run_*/logs.parquet', union_by_name=true) l

SQL INSERT INTO ab_raw
            WITH seg AS MATERIALIZED (
              SELECT ANY_VALUE(b) b, ANY_VALUE(frm) frm, ANY_VALUE(t2) t2, ANY_VALUE(v) v
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 8 = 0
                AND (frm IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) OR t2 IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs))
              GROUP BY tag, tx, li),
            d AS (
              SELECT t2 AS a, b, CAST(v AS HUGEINT) AS d FROM seg
              UNION ALL
              SELECT frm, b, -CAST(v AS HUGEINT) FROM seg WHERE frm <> '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000')
            SELECT a, b, SUM(d) FROM d WHERE a IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) GROUP BY a, b

SQL WITH g AS (
              SELECT tag, tx, li, COUNT(*) n, COUNT(DISTINCT (b, ts, frm, t2, v)) variants
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 8 = 1 GROUP BY tag, tx, li)

SQL INSERT INTO ab_raw
            WITH seg AS MATERIALIZED (
              SELECT ANY_VALUE(b) b, ANY_VALUE(frm) frm, ANY_VALUE(t2) t2, ANY_VALUE(v) v
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 8 = 1
                AND (frm IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) OR t2 IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs))
              GROUP BY tag, tx, li),
            d AS (
              SELECT t2 AS a, b, CAST(v AS HUGEINT) AS d FROM seg
              UNION ALL
              SELECT frm, b, -CAST(v AS HUGEINT) FROM seg WHERE frm <> '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000')
            SELECT a, b, SUM(d) FROM d WHERE a IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) GROUP BY a, b

CATALOG {"tables": [["ab"], ["only_addrs"]], "views": [["raw_rows"]]}
SQL SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ab
SQL CREATE TABLE peaks AS
            WITH cum AS (SELECT a, b, SUM(dd) OVER (PARTITION BY a ORDER BY b) c FROM ab),
                 mx AS (SELECT a, MAX(c) mc FROM cum GROUP BY a HAVING MAX(c) > 0)
            SELECT m.a, m.mc, MIN(cum.b) pb FROM mx m
            JOIN cum ON cum.a = m.a AND cum.c = m.mc GROUP BY m.a, m.mc
SQL SELECT a, mc, pb FROM peaks

SQL SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
        SELECT l.block_number b,
               COALESCE(strftime(make_timestamp((bt.ts_i * 1000000)::BIGINT), '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S'), '') ts,
               lower(l.transaction_hash) tx, l.log_index li,
               '0x' || right(lower(COALESCE(l.topic1, repeat('0', 64))), 40) frm,
               '0x' || right(lower(COALESCE(l.topic2, repeat('0', 64))), 40) t2,
               CASE WHEN l.data IS NULL OR l.data IN ('', '0x') THEN '0' ELSE CAST(('0x'||substr(data,35,16))::UBIGINT::HUGEINT * '18446744073709551616'::HUGEINT + ('0x'||substr(data,51,16))::UBIGINT::HUGEINT AS VARCHAR) END v,
               't' tag
        FROM read_parquet('/private/var/folders/z6/mlppyd097493blf6v8_h29dw0000gp/T/w1-zw0vnb4p/main/v2/run_*/logs.parquet', union_by_name=true) l
        LEFT JOIN (SELECT number, TRY_CAST(ANY_VALUE(timestamp) AS UBIGINT) ts_i
                   FROM read_parquet('/private/var/folders/z6/mlppyd097493blf6v8_h29dw0000gp/T/w1-zw0vnb4p/main/v2/run_*/blocks.parquet', union_by_name=true)
                   WHERE number IS NOT NULL GROUP BY number) bt
               ON bt.number = l.block_number
        WHERE l.block_number IS NOT NULL AND l.log_index IS NOT NULL
          AND (l.data IS NULL OR l.data IN ('', '0x') OR LENGTH(l.data) = 66)
          AND l.block_number >= 100 AND l.block_number < 2000100)
SQL CREATE VIEW raw_rows AS 
        SELECT l.block_number b,
               COALESCE(strftime(make_timestamp((bt.ts_i * 1000000)::BIGINT), '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S'), '') ts,
               lower(l.transaction_hash) tx, l.log_index li,
               '0x' || right(lower(COALESCE(l.topic1, repeat('0', 64))), 40) frm,
               '0x' || right(lower(COALESCE(l.topic2, repeat('0', 64))), 40) t2,
               CASE WHEN l.data IS NULL OR l.data IN ('', '0x') THEN '0' ELSE CAST(('0x'||substr(data,35,16))::UBIGINT::HUGEINT * '18446744073709551616'::HUGEINT + ('0x'||substr(data,51,16))::UBIGINT::HUGEINT AS VARCHAR) END v,
               't' tag
        FROM read_parquet('/private/var/folders/z6/mlppyd097493blf6v8_h29dw0000gp/T/w1-zw0vnb4p/main/v2/run_*/logs.parquet', union_by_name=true) l
        LEFT JOIN (SELECT number, TRY_CAST(ANY_VALUE(timestamp) AS UBIGINT) ts_i
                   FROM read_parquet('/private/var/folders/z6/mlppyd097493blf6v8_h29dw0000gp/T/w1-zw0vnb4p/main/v2/run_*/blocks.parquet', union_by_name=true)
                   WHERE number IS NOT NULL GROUP BY number) bt
               ON bt.number = l.block_number
        WHERE l.block_number IS NOT NULL AND l.log_index IS NOT NULL
          AND (l.data IS NULL OR l.data IN ('', '0x') OR LENGTH(l.data) = 66)
          AND l.block_number >= 100 AND l.block_number < 2000100

SQL INSERT INTO ab_raw
            WITH seg AS MATERIALIZED (
              SELECT ANY_VALUE(b) b, ANY_VALUE(frm) frm, ANY_VALUE(t2) t2, ANY_VALUE(v) v
              FROM raw_rows WHERE hash(tag, tx, li) % 1 = 0
                AND (frm IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) OR t2 IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs))
              GROUP BY tag, tx, li),
            d AS (
              SELECT t2 AS a, b, CAST(v AS HUGEINT) AS d FROM seg
              UNION ALL
              SELECT frm, b, -CAST(v AS HUGEINT) FROM seg WHERE frm <> '0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000')
            SELECT a, b, SUM(d) FROM d WHERE a IN (SELECT a FROM only_addrs) GROUP BY a, b

CATALOG {"tables": [["ab_raw"], ["only_addrs"]], "views": [["raw_rows"]]}
SQL SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ab_raw
SQL CREATE TABLE ab AS SELECT a, b, SUM(dd) dd FROM ab_raw GROUP BY a, b
SQL DROP TABLE ab_raw
CATALOG {"tables": [["ab"], ["only_addrs"]], "views": [["raw_rows"]]}
SQL SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ab
SQL CREATE TABLE peaks AS
            WITH cum AS (SELECT a, b, SUM(dd) OVER (PARTITION BY a ORDER BY b) c FROM ab),
                 mx AS (SELECT a, MAX(c) mc FROM cum GROUP BY a HAVING MAX(c) > 0)
            SELECT m.a, m.mc, MIN(cum.b) pb FROM mx m
            JOIN cum ON cum.a = m.a AND cum.c = m.mc GROUP BY m.a, m.mc
SQL SELECT a, mc, pb FROM peaks
